# Predestination in the Parables of Jesus

**[00:00:00]** The parables of Jesus are some of the most interesting and enjoyable parts of the New

**[00:00:08]** Testament, and they have a lot to teach us about the kingdom and God's plan of redemption.

**[00:00:12]** Today we're going to look at many parables and see some important lessons on predestination.

**[00:00:30]** So let's get started.
